## APPENDIX — THE BUILD, VERBATIM (the scratchpad copies at `scratchpad/cch/` are the working ones; `brackets.json` is regenerated by `brackets.py` + `readcopy.py`)

### `head.py`

```python
KIT='settlement-engine-kits/fp-final-a73343ed-2026-10-17'
HEADER = '''# DESIGN_FP_ARCH_CH — THE CHAMPIONS: champions and villains, the most agentic persons in the world, introduced by the DM and answered by the world

## Compiled on the owner's word of 2026-10-17 in the FP final architecture program (kit `settlement-engine-kits/fp-final-a73343ed-2026-10-17/`) and amended in place with THE PATRON DEITY on the owner's word of 2026-10-20; DARK behind six virtual flags (§19); nothing scheduled.
## CONSOLIDATED 2026-10-20 from the bracketed text of record at `1beabdc85` — the review trail is in git and in `settlement-engine-kits/fp-final-a73343ed-2026-10-17/history/CH-cure-record.md`; the compile log and its measurements (taken at master `81f1cd963`) are the kit's `cure/L-CH.md`.
## Bound by the FP constitution's requirements 1–24 (`DESIGN_FP_SPINE.md` §1; 15–24's text of record is `DESIGN_FP_COHERENCE_FOLD.md` §1) and by the coherence fold. The OWNER's shapes and rows are marked ⭐; every ruling is vetoable (§23).

---

## THE READER'S GUIDE

1. WHAT THIS IS. A champion or a villain — code token **`paragon`** (`champion` is landed); prose words champion, villain and the sixteen display nouns — is a person the DM alone introduces (CH1), carried as ONE conditional mark on the world-NPC ledger (CH2), who each week CHOOSES whether to stay or go and whether to use its gift where it stands (§4), and whose gift leans ONE landed seam by a bounded amount, its villain leaning the same seam the other way (CH5, CH6, §6). It may be bound to a court or free (§7), barred at a town's gate (§11), sworn to a god (§10b), and fails by degrees, never by death (§12).
2. ITS ONE OWNER. This volume owns the `paragon` mark and its normalizer (`src/domain/paragon/`), the weekly choice, the gifts' rows and the closed `PARAGON_LEAN_SEAMS`, the retainer, the town's-gate default `gateDefault` for every actor kind, the court's belief of a hidden employer (P3 `paragonBeliefs`), THE CORPS' arm of the mark and THE PATRON DEITY's one lean `swornLean`.
3. WHAT IT READS. BELIEF for everything a paragon or a court decides about another: the settlement's beliefs plus a capped carried picture (§4.6), RN's fame and regard through the rumour road, `readBeliefRelationship`, `believedWhereabouts`, `believedEmployerOf`; TRUTH only in the omniscient arm and for a deciding court's own facts (CH7, §2.2 `gateDefault`).
4. WHAT IT WRITES, one writer each: the ledger record through `moveNpcRecord` (the ledger's one writer); deeds only through RN's `mintDeed` / `strikeDeed` (§2.2b); P3 through CH's pass in THE ONE PERSON STAGE; bans as the ledger's own `exclusions` (CH12).
5. WHERE IT MOUNTS. Its pass REGISTERS in RN's one pre-return mount in `envoyPulse.js :: advanceEnvoyDiplomacyPulse` (THE ONE PERSON STAGE); its birth-side rows in `stressorGates.js :: STRESSOR_SPAWN_GATES`; its run-side leans at seams that already take `worldState` (`religiousContest.js :: advanceReligionStates`, `pactFormation.js :: reserveFor`, `pestilenceKernel.js :: advanceSettlementPestilence`, `warPeaceDecision.js :: readWarPeaceDecision`); its legs through `npcCirculationTransit.js :: planWanderLeg` → `namedPersonTransit.js :: openNamedPersonLeg`; zero lines in `pulseKernel.js`, `applyWorldPulse.js` and `warTermination.js` (CH17).
6. FLAGS: `paragonsEnabled` · `paragonGiftsEnabled` · `paragonRetainersEnabled` · `paragonGateEnabled` · `paragonCovertEnabled` · `paragonCorpsEnabled` (§19), every one premium. HEARING KINDS: `retainer_offer` · `paragon_resigns` · `champion_duel` · `person_at_the_gate` · `person_banned` · `banished_found` · `paragon_disgraced` · `polarity_turn` · `never_line_tested` (§18); no custody kind. CHAIN: C12 THE CHAMPIONS in the fold's §6, waves CH-0 to CH-11 (§20).
7. DEPENDS ON: RN (fame, regard, the deed ledger), the world-NPC ledger (W-H1/H3), ES (custody ES-2b; the errand-row slot), PL (crown reach, the crown's ban), WC (free units), THE BODIES (BD), ST, PR, PS, OP, MN, HB's habit curve, W-LIVES, W-COIN and the generals design (C4). READ BY: PT (`gateDefault`), RN (the `paragon_act` registration), BD (`isWardenParagon`, `securityOfficeOf`, the one leader record), PR and MN (the lean seams), OP and PS.
8. THE WORDS NOT TO CONFUSE: `champion` the polarity value versus the landed pending-edit op `'champion-npc'`; `swornDeity` the paragon's oath versus `patronDeity`, the SETTLEMENT's seated patron; "the town's gate" (admission) versus "a stressor gate" (`STRESSOR_SPAWN_GATES`) — "gate" is never written bare (§2.3's map register).

'''
OWNER_DOCTRINES = None  # built from bracket 2's tail in build.py
# F4 (consolidate/CH-verify.md) — THE READER'S GUIDE item 3 restored to the gateDefault row's full law (B115: the crown's proclaimed ban is the one public fact of another court read as truth)
_a="TRUTH only in the omniscient arm and for a deciding court's own facts (CH7, §2.2 `gateDefault`)."
_b="TRUTH only in the omniscient arm, for a deciding court's own facts, and for ONE public fact of another court — the crown's proclaimed ban at a member (CH7, §2.2 `gateDefault`, §11 step 2)."
assert HEADER.count(_a)==1; HEADER=HEADER.replace(_a,_b)
```

### `build.py`

```python
import json,re,sys,os
CCH=os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
OLD='/Users/cstokes/Desktop/settlement-engine-kits/fp-final-a73343ed-2026-10-17/history/CH-bracketed-1beabdc85.md'
S=open(OLD,encoding='utf-8').read(); L=S.split('\n')
BR=json.load(open(CCH+'/brackets.json'))
X={}; DR={}; MV={}; SUBS=[]; LINE={}; ARROW_FOLD={}; INSERT_AFTER={}; KEEP={}; NOTE={}
for f in sorted(x for x in os.listdir(CCH) if re.match(r'd\d\d\.py$',x)): exec(open(CCH+'/'+f).read())
exec(open(CCH+'/head.py').read())
END=int(sys.argv[1]) if len(sys.argv)>1 else 1115
def content(i,mode):
    t=BR[i]['text'][1:-1]; k=t.index(' — '); r=t[k+3:]
    if mode=='colon':
        j=r.find(': '); r=r[j+2:] if j>=0 else r
    return r
LINE[1]=HEADER.rstrip('\n')
DOC=content(2,'dash')
INSERT_AFTER[34]='\nAnd on 2026-10-18 — '+DOC
offs=[0]
for l in L: offs.append(offs[-1]+len(l)+1)
# bracket replacement per line
byline={}
for b in BR: byline.setdefault(b['line'],[]).append(b)
def rep(i,atstart):
    v=X.get(i,'')
    if i in DR or i in MV: v=''
    if v is None: v=''
    if isinstance(v,tuple):
        mode=v[1]; subs=v[2]; pre=v[3] if len(v)>3 else ('' if atstart else ' ')
        c=content(i,mode)
        for a,bb in subs:
            assert c.count(a)==1,(i,a); c=c.replace(a,bb)
        v=pre+c
    return v
out=[]; touched=set(); NL={}
for n in range(1,END+1):
    l=L[n-1]
    if n in byline:
        ls=offs[n-1]; seg=[];p=0
        for b in byline[n]:
            st=b['start']-ls; en=b['end']-ls
            pre=l[p:st]; atstart=(pre.strip()=='' and p==0)
            v=rep(b['i'],atstart)
            if v=='' :
                nxt=l[en:en+1]
                if pre.endswith(' ') and (nxt in ('',' ','.',',',';',':',')','|') ): pre=pre[:-1]
                elif atstart and nxt==' ': en+=1
            seg.append(pre); seg.append(v); p=en
        seg.append(l[p:]); l=''.join(seg); touched.add(n)
        if l.startswith('|'): l=re.sub(r'\|(\s*)\|',lambda m:'| — |',l); l=re.sub(r'\|(\s*)\|',lambda m:'| — |',l)
    for (sn,a,bb) in SUBS:
        if sn==n:
            assert l.count(a)==1,(n,a[:80],l.count(a)); l=l.replace(a,bb)
    if n in LINE:
        if LINE[n] is None: NL[n]=None; continue
        l=LINE[n]
    l=l.rstrip(' ')
    NL[n]=l
    out.append(l)
    if n in INSERT_AFTER: out.append(INSERT_AFTER[n])
incode=False
for k,l in enumerate(out):
    if l.startswith('```'): incode=not incode; continue
    if not incode and '\n' not in l: out[k]=re.sub(r'(?<=\S)  +(?=\S)',' ',l)
txt='\n'.join(out)
txt=re.sub(r'\n{3,}','\n\n',txt)
json.dump(NL,open(CCH+'/newlines.json','w'),ensure_ascii=False)
open(CCH+'/body_new.md','w').write(txt+'\n')
print('lines',len(out),'bytes',len(txt.encode()))
```

### `ledger.py`

```python
import json,re,sys,os
CCH=os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
OLD='/Users/cstokes/Desktop/settlement-engine-kits/fp-final-a73343ed-2026-10-17/history/CH-bracketed-1beabdc85.md'
S=open(OLD,encoding='utf-8').read(); L=S.split('\n')
BR=json.load(open(CCH+'/brackets.json'))
X={}; DR={}; MV={}; SUBS=[]; LINE={}; ARROW_FOLD={}; INSERT_AFTER={}; KEEP={}; NOTE={}
for f in sorted(x for x in os.listdir(CCH) if re.match(r'd\d\d\.py$',x)): exec(open(CCH+'/'+f).read())
END=int(sys.argv[1]); NL={int(k):v for k,v in json.load(open(CCH+'/newlines.json')).items()}
# section of each old line
sec={};cur='header'
for i,l in enumerate(L,1):
    m=re.match(r'^(#{2,3}) (.*)',l)
    if m and i>=18:
        t=m.group(2); mm=re.match(r'(§\d+b?|\d+\.\d+b?)\b',t)
        cur=('§'+mm.group(1) if mm and not mm.group(1).startswith('§') else mm.group(1)) if mm else t[:28]
    sec[i]=cur
offs=[0]
for l in L: offs.append(offs[-1]+len(l)+1)
inbr=[False]*len(S)
for b in BR:
    for k in range(b['start'],b['end']): inbr[k]=True
CURE0=1116
rows=[]
def add(kind,line,text,disp,new,what): rows.append((kind,line,text,disp,new,what))
def first(t): return t[:60].replace('|','\\|').replace('`',"'")
stampre=re.compile(r'^\[(CURED|REFINED|ADDED|RE-POINTED|OPENED) \d{4}-\d\d-\d\d \([^()]*\)( — [^:\]]{0,90})?\]$')
for b in BR:
    i=b['i'];n=b['line'];t=b['text']
    if n>END: add('B%03d'%i,n,first(t),'PENDING','—','section not yet consolidated'); continue
    if i in MV: add('B%03d'%i,n,first(t),'MOVED','history',MV[i]); continue
    if n in MV_LINES: add('B%03d'%i,n,first(t),'FOLDED' if i==2 else 'MOVED','THE OWNER\'S WORDS' if i==2 else 'history','the doctrines of 2026-10-18 and the owner\'s 06:35 question folded verbatim as a paragraph of THE OWNER\'S WORDS' if i==2 else 'inside a header/status line moved to history Part 1'); continue
    if i in DR: s,note=DR[i]; add('B%03d'%i,n,first(t),'DROPPED','—','superseded by B%03d: %s'%(s,note)); continue
    ns=sec[n]
    if n in ARROW_FOLD: add('B%03d'%i,n,first(t),'FOLDED',ns,'the stamp of a ↳ row folded into its parent row (old line %d)'%ARROW_FOLD[n]); continue
    if i in NOTE: add('B%03d'%i,n,first(t),'FOLDED',ns,NOTE[i]); continue
    if i in X and X[i] not in ('',None): add('B%03d'%i,n,first(t),'FOLDED',ns,'its content folded into the sentence or cell it amended (latest ruling as stated)'); continue
    if i in X: add('B%03d'%i,n,first(t),'FOLDED',ns,'its content folded by the line rewrite of old line %d'%n); continue
    if b.get('stamp'): add('B%03d'%i,n,first(t),'FOLDED',ns,'a stamp: the cured text stands at the same place; the ids stay in the text of record'); continue
    add('B%03d'%i,n,first(t),'FOLDED',ns,'a change note: the text at the same place already carries the change; the note stays in the text of record')
# cure-record brackets
for m in re.finditer(r'\[(CURED|REFINED|ADDED|RE-POINTED|OPENED|STOP|CORRECTED|AMENDED|RULED)\b',S[offs[CURE0-1]:]):
    p=offs[CURE0-1]+m.start(); n=next(k for k in range(1,len(offs)) if offs[k]>p)
    add('C%02d'%(len([r for r in rows if r[0].startswith('C')])+1),n,first(S[p:p+60]),'MOVED','history','inside §25 THE CURE RECORD, moved verbatim to history Part 2')
# arrows, rounds, stops, fp-final stamps outside brackets
pats=[('A','↳'),('H',r'\bROUND \d[ab]?\b|\bRound \d[ab]?\b|\bround r?\d[ab]?\b'),('S',r'STOP'),('F',r'\(fp-final[^)]*\)')]
cnt={}
for n in range(1,len(L)+1):
    l=L[n-1]; base=offs[n-1]
    for k,pat in pats:
        for m in re.finditer(pat,l):
            if inbr[base+m.start()]: continue
            cnt[k]=cnt.get(k,0)+1; rid='%s%02d'%(k,cnt[k])
            ctx=first(l[max(0,m.start()-20):m.start()+40])
            if n>=CURE0: add(rid,n,ctx,'MOVED','history','inside §25 THE CURE RECORD, moved verbatim to history Part 2'); continue
            if n in MV_LINES: add(rid,n,ctx,'MOVED','history','inside a header/status line moved to history Part 1 (%s)'%MV_LINES[n]); continue
            if n>END: add(rid,n,ctx,'PENDING','—','section not yet consolidated'); continue
            nl=NL.get(n)
            if k=='A':
                add(rid,n,ctx,'FOLDED',sec[n],('the ↳ row folded into its parent row (old line %d); the ↳ line removed'%ARROW_FOLD[n]) if n in ARROW_FOLD else ('the ↳ reference removed; the row is cited by its name' if (nl is not None and '↳' not in nl) else 'NO DISPOSITION')); continue
            if nl is not None and re.search(pat,nl):
                add(rid,n,ctx,'KEPT',sec[n],'KEPT — CHECK'); continue
            add(rid,n,ctx,'FOLDED',sec[n],'the label removed; the passage stands in final form')
for n,why in MV_LINES.items():
    add('L%02d'%n,n,first(L[n-1]),'MOVED','history','header/status line moved to history Part 1: '+why)
# cure record headers
for n in range(CURE0,len(L)+1):
    if L[n-1].startswith('## §25') or L[n-1].startswith('### '): add('R%02d'%(n-CURE0+1),n,first(L[n-1]),'MOVED','history','cure-record header, moved verbatim to history Part 2')
from collections import Counter
c=Counter(r[3] for r in rows)
print('rows',len(rows),dict(c))
bad=[r for r in rows if 'CHECK' in r[5] or 'NO DISPOSITION' in r[5]]
for r in bad: print('CHECK',r)
json.dump(rows,open(CCH+'/ledger_rows.json','w'),ensure_ascii=False)
```

# renders ledger_rows.json (ledger.py's output) as §7's markdown table: old section from the old text, new § from the row
import json,os,re
CCH=os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
OLD='/Users/cstokes/Desktop/settlement-engine-kits/fp-final-a73343ed-2026-10-17/history/CH-bracketed-1beabdc85.md'
L=open(OLD,encoding='utf-8').read().split('\n')
sec={};cur='header'
for i,l in enumerate(L,1):
    m=re.match(r'^(#{2,3}) (.*)',l)
    if m and i>=18:
        t=m.group(2); mm=re.match(r'(§\d+b?|\d+\.\d+b?)\b',t)
        cur=('§'+mm.group(1) if mm and not mm.group(1).startswith('§') else mm.group(1)) if mm else t[:28]
    sec[i]=cur
OW="THE OWNER'S WORDS (2026-10-1"
rows=json.load(open(CCH+'/ledger_rows.json'))
out=['## 7. THE ROWS (one per marker; `B` = body bracket by index in old-text order · `C` = cure-record bracket · `A` = `↳` outside brackets · `H` = round word outside brackets · `S` = `STOP` outside brackets · `F` = `(fp-final …)` stamp outside brackets · `L` = header/status line · `R` = cure-record header)','','| id | old line | old section | first characters | disposition | new § | what happened |','|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|']
for (rid,line,first,disp,new,what) in rows:
    if what=='KEPT — CHECK': what='KEPT as a design mark: the DM\'s verb "STOP the hunt" (requirement 24), not a review marker'
    sl=sec[line].replace(OW,"THE OWNER'S WORDS"); new=new.replace(OW,"THE OWNER'S WORDS")
    out.append('| %s | L%d | %s | `%s` | %s | %s | %s |'%(rid,line,sl,first,disp,new,what.replace('|','\\|')))
open(CCH+'/ledger_rows_final.md','w').write('\n'.join(out)+'\n')

pats=[r'↳',r'\bSTOP\b',r'fp-final',r'\b[Rr]ound\b',r'\bROUND\b',r'\br\d[ab]?\b',r'\bfinding',r'\bskeptic',r'\bthe chair\b',r"\bchair's\b",r'\blane\b',r'\bverifier',r'RULINGS',r'\b[A-Z]{2,5}(-[A-Za-z]+)?-r\d[ab]?-\d+',r'\b(CH|PL|MN|RN|PT|PS|ST|PR|BD|OP|MG|WS|FS|WC|SEAMS|HOST)-(V|W|X|PD-V)\d+',r'\bP5C?-\d+',r'\b[A-H]\.\d{1,2}\b',r'\bCHAIR-NOTES',r'\brouted\b|\bROUTED\b',r'\b20\d\d-\d\d-\d\d\b',r'\bcure/|\bskeptic/|\bbriefs/',r'\bthis round\b',r'\bP6c?\b',r'\bwithdrawn\b|\bWITHDRAWN\b|\bSTRUCK\b|\bstruck\b']

### `brackets.py`

```python
import re,sys,json,bisect
OLD='/Users/cstokes/Desktop/settlement-engine-kits/fp-final-a73343ed-2026-10-17/history/CH-bracketed-1beabdc85.md'
s=open(OLD,encoding='utf-8').read()
lines=s.split('\n')
offs=[0]
for l in lines: offs.append(offs[-1]+len(l)+1)
def lineof(pos): return bisect.bisect_right(offs,pos)
BODY_END=offs[1115]  # end of line 1115
out=[]
pos=0
pat=re.compile(r'\[(CURED|REFINED|ADDED|RE-POINTED|OPENED|RE-RULED|STOP|CORRECTED|AMENDED|RULED|NOTED)\b')
i=0
while True:
    m=pat.search(s,i)
    if not m or m.start()>=BODY_END: break
    st=m.start(); depth=0; j=st
    while j<len(s):
        c=s[j]
        if c=='[': depth+=1
        elif c==']':
            depth-=1
            if depth==0: break
        j+=1
    out.append(dict(i=len(out),start=st,end=j+1,line=lineof(st),endline=lineof(j),kind=m.group(1),text=s[st:j+1]))
    i=j+1
json.dump(out,open('brackets.json','w'),ensure_ascii=False,indent=0)
print('n',len(out),'bytes',sum(len(b['text'].encode()) for b in out))
from collections import Counter
print(Counter(b['kind'] for b in out))
print('multiline',sum(1 for b in out if b['line']!=b['endline']))
ln=sorted(len(b['text']) for b in out)
print('len pctl',[ln[int(len(ln)*q)] for q in (0.1,0.25,0.5,0.75,0.9,0.99)], ln[-1])
# nested markers inside brackets
nested=sum(1 for b in out if pat.search(b['text'],1))
print('brackets containing another marker',nested)
```

import json,re
OLD='/Users/cstokes/Desktop/settlement-engine-kits/fp-final-a73343ed-2026-10-17/history/CH-bracketed-1beabdc85.md'

### `plain.py`

```python
import json,re
OLD='/Users/cstokes/Desktop/settlement-engine-kits/fp-final-a73343ed-2026-10-17/history/CH-bracketed-1beabdc85.md'
s=open(OLD,encoding='utf-8').read()
B=json.load(open('brackets.json'))
# plain = body with brackets replaced by \x00<i>\x00 placeholders
out=[];p=0
for b in B:
    out.append(s[p:b['start']]); out.append('⟦%d⟧'%b['i']); p=b['end']
out.append(s[p:])
t=''.join(out)
open('withph.md','w').write(t)
L=t.split('\n')
body=L[:1115]
open('body_ph.md','w').write('\n'.join(body))
# unbracketed markers in body
for i,l in enumerate(body,1):
    ls=re.sub(r'⟦\d+⟧','',l)
    hits=[]
    for pat in [r'↳',r'STOP',r'\(fp-final[^)]*\)',r'\bround r?\d[ab]?\b',r'\bRound\b',r'\bfinding',r'\bskeptic',r'\bthe chair\b',r'\bthe lane\b',r'\bverifier']:
        for m in re.finditer(pat,ls,flags=re.I if pat.startswith(r'\bround') else 0):
            hits.append(m.group(0))
    if hits: print(i, hits)

The champions brief's word, verbatim: "they can be taken ransomed depending on their role and situation and areas traveled". What does NOT change until that wave lands, and is not widened by the opening: the DM's hand (`authored.heldAt`) stays every world's only hold; the failure ladder still tops at `disgrace` (no capture rung is designed here — a rung would be new design); §11's road arm keeps `admitted · refused` (a body's TAKING of a paragon is THE BODIES' act on ES's hold, BD §9.10, never a new CH verdict); `banished_found` keeps `expelled · let_be`.

*What the code carries* (CONFIRMED at `81f1cd963`): `foreignGuestHold.js :: normalizeForeignGuestHold` refuses any row without an errand and an encounter (`if (!id || !npcId || !errandId || !encounterId || !captorId || !venueId …) return null;`), its one opener is `envoyInterceptionStage.js :: holdInterruptedEnvoy`, and ES-2 recorded why nobody else may open it: "The errand DTO refuses the held state for a row that carries no army-carried ENCOUNTER … Relaxing either shape is a persisted-schema question and this estate gates those on the owner" (`custodyWritten: false`). *The law:* a held paragon's custody WAITS ON ES-2b's custody wave (fold §3.3.13, OD-FC-60), and CH keeps NO landed custody arm as live design: no `jailed` (the H2 local verdict, `npcVerdictTable.js :: HOLDING_VERDICT`), no `paragon.held`, no `seized`, no second custody road. *Until ES-2b:* THE DM'S HAND is the only hold — the DM brings the paragon where it is held (`assign`) and writes `authored.heldAt` through the `introduce` revision; while it stands, the paragon neither moves nor exerts (§4.2), its page and its town's dossier say "held at <town>" in the DM's word, and it is released when the DM's revision clears the field. It is AUTHORED WHEREABOUTS, never an engine hold: no captor court, no ransom, no hearing (the DM's own act needs none), no custody receipt. The owner's §125.5 (a free paragon taken by bandits, released with no ransom) is the DM's to tell the same way.

*When ES-2b's custody wave lands* (LIVE DESIGN, above): a held paragon rides the generalized hold; the ransom is the registry's `ransom` card, the court's choices in its weeks `pay · hunt · wait` (the fold's `ransom` row, CLOCKED), the employer's answer read against RN's court regard of the paragon; the landed `ransomChoices.js :: HOME_ANSWERS` `pay · refuse · abandon` ("`Object.freeze(['pay', 'refuse', 'abandon'])`", CONFIRMED at `81f1cd963`) stay the court-to-court leaf's internal answers, never the card's words (its `refuse` and `abandon` only delay), and the leaf's `abandon`, where recorded, still marks the landed grievance `abandoned_to_captivity` (the fold §3.3.13) — CH its first consumer; the held villain's nature decides whether it talks (§9, road 4); the ransom's PERSISTENCE stays WR-7d's. A CORPS has no custody at all — it is defeated or disbanded (§16b).

*A paragon taken by a body* (THE BODIES' items 34 and 34b: "a NAMED person taken by a body (an envoy, a paragon; never the party …) is CUSTODY, which WAITS ON ES-2b"; the owner's ransom race, BOARD, the 18:54 stamp, verbatim: "this also changes the ransom rules if they are taken by a gang or conclave. if the gang can survive with teh NPC for long, then they will have the ransom granted, if they are defeated, then the captive goes free."): a BODY takes no paragon until ES-2b's wave lands (§11's road arm turns it back, nothing more). The outcomes are `ransom_granted · captive_freed · captive_released` (item 34b's closed words; the fold's custody rows), and the three closings are a PARTITION: `ransom_granted` the body SURVIVED the clock (the automatic resolution pays) or the court paid early; `captive_freed` the body was defeated first; `captive_released` ONLY the body's own act when its sustenance cannot hold the captive (it lets him go early, receipted) or the DM's decision — never the clock's automatic outcome. A FREE paragon's captors find no court to ransom it to (ODQ §125.5: "NO ransom is issued (no settlement tie to ransom against)"), so NO ransom clock runs — there is no treasury the automatic resolution could pay from — and the body holds the paragon until its sustenance cannot (it lets him go, `captive_released`, the body's own act, receipted), it is defeated (`captive_freed`) or the DM decides (vetoable: this reads the free paragon's case as no clock at all). ## LENS → READINGS (2026-10-23, the chair's ruling on the fold §5 `lens` row)
